# AGENTS.md

MetaNC is under active development. Favor clarity, small changes, and documentation that helps future contributors understand decisions quickly.

    def test_cli_print_payload_honors_json_flag(self) -> None:
        buffer = io.StringIO()
        with redirect_stdout(buffer):
            _print_payload({"alpha": "one", "beta": {"nested": 2}}, as_json=False)
        self.assertEqual(
            buffer.getvalue().splitlines(),
            [
                "alpha: one",
                'beta: {"nested": 2}',
            ],
        )

    def test_generated_outputs_match_snapshots(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            web_files = generate_web(self.bundle, Path(temp_dir) / "web")
            qml_files = generate_qml(self.bundle, Path(temp_dir) / "qml")

            self.assertEqual(
                Path(web_files["index.html"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                (SNAPSHOTS / "web" / "index.html.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                Path(web_files["styles.css"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                (SNAPSHOTS / "web" / "styles.css.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                Path(web_files["app.js"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                (SNAPSHOTS / "web" / "app.js.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                Path(web_files["runtime.js"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                (SNAPSHOTS / "web" / "runtime.js.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                Path(qml_files["Main.qml"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                (SNAPSHOTS / "qml" / "Main.qml.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                Path(qml_files["ThemeStore.js"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
                (SNAPSHOTS / "qml" / "ThemeStore.js.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),

                (SNAPSHOTS / "qml" / "RuntimeStore.qml.snap").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
            )

def _find_node(node: dict, node_id: str) -> dict | None:
    if node.get("id") == node_id:
        return node
    for child in node.get("children", []) or []:
        if isinstance(child, dict):
            found = _find_node(child, node_id)
            if found is not None:
                return found
    return None


def _find_page(ir: dict, page_id: str) -> dict:
    return next(page for page in ir["pages"] if page["id"] == page_id)

async function waitForExpectations(browserController, expectations, timeoutMs, label) {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
  let lastSnapshot = null;
  let lastFailure = "";
  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
    lastSnapshot = await runtimeSnapshot(browserController);
    const result = expectationsSatisfied(lastSnapshot, expectations || {});
    if (result.ok) {
      return lastSnapshot;
    }
    lastFailure = result.reason;
    await delay(100);
  }
  const snapshotText = JSON.stringify(lastSnapshot, null, 2);
  throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label}: ${lastFailure}\nsnapshot=${snapshotText}`);

"""Static Web prototype generator based on the normalized IR."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from ..shared.common import (
    BRAND_MARK_RELATIVE_PATH,
    BRAND_MARK_SOURCE_PATH,
    copy_generated_files,
    ensure_directory,
    prepare_generation_payload,
    write_generated_files,
)
from .app_shell import build_app_js, build_web_runtime_ir
from .bundler import BUNDLE_RELATIVE_PATH, build_web_client_bundle, web_client_source_digest
from .runtime_shell import build_runtime_config_js, build_runtime_js
from .styles import build_styles_css
from .shell import build_index_html, build_readme
from tools.hmi_dsl.compiler.loader import PackageBundle


def generate_web(bundle: PackageBundle, output_dir: str | Path, theme_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
    payload = prepare_generation_payload(bundle, theme_id=theme_id)
    directory = ensure_directory(output_dir)

    styles_css = build_styles_css(payload)
    config_js = build_runtime_config_js()
    runtime_js = build_runtime_js(payload)
    app_js = build_app_js(payload)
    model_json = _build_model_json(payload)
    runtime_seed_json = _build_runtime_seed_json(payload)
    web_client_bundle = build_web_client_bundle()
    asset_version = hashlib.sha256(
        "\n".join(
            [
                styles_css,
                config_js,
                runtime_js,
                app_js,
                model_json,
                runtime_seed_json,
                web_client_source_digest(),
            ]
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()[:12]

    files = {
        "index.html": build_index_html(payload, asset_version=asset_version),
        "styles.css": styles_css,
        "config.js": config_js,
        "runtime.js": runtime_js,
        "app.js": app_js,
        "model.generated.json": model_json,
        "runtime_seed.generated.json": runtime_seed_json,
        BUNDLE_RELATIVE_PATH: web_client_bundle,
        "README.md": build_readme(payload),
    }

    written = write_generated_files(directory, files)
    if BRAND_MARK_SOURCE_PATH.exists():
        written.update(copy_generated_files(directory, {BRAND_MARK_RELATIVE_PATH: BRAND_MARK_SOURCE_PATH}))
    return written


def _build_model_json(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return (
        json.dumps(
            {
                "active_theme_id": payload["active_theme_id"],
                "ir": build_web_runtime_ir(payload["ir"]),
                "settings_catalog": payload.get("settings_catalog", {}),
                "theme_options": payload["theme_options"],
                "themes": payload["themes"],
            },
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
        + "\n"
    )


def _build_runtime_seed_json(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return (
        json.dumps(
            {
                "program_workspace": payload["program_workspace"],
                "runtime_plan": payload["runtime_plan"],
                "runtime_seed": payload["runtime_seed"],
            },
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
        + "\n"
    )

"""Runtime shell builders for generated web artifacts."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any

from .runtime_fragments import RUNTIME_JS_TEMPLATE


def build_runtime_config_js(server_mode: str = "hybrid", server_base_url: str = "") -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        [
            "window.HMI_RUNTIME_CONFIG = Object.assign({}, window.HMI_RUNTIME_CONFIG || {}, {",
            f"  serverMode: {json.dumps(str(server_mode or 'hybrid'))},",
            f"  serverBaseUrl: {json.dumps(str(server_base_url or ''))},",
            "});",
            "",
        ]
    )


def build_runtime_js(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    runtime_plan_json = json.dumps(payload["runtime_plan"], indent=2, sort_keys=True)
    runtime_seed_json = json.dumps(payload["runtime_seed"], indent=2, sort_keys=True)
    program_workspace_json = json.dumps(payload["program_workspace"], indent=2, sort_keys=True)
    return (
        RUNTIME_JS_TEMPLATE.replace("__RUNTIME_PLAN_JSON__", runtime_plan_json)
        .replace("__RUNTIME_SEED_JSON__", runtime_seed_json)
        .replace("__PROGRAM_WORKSPACE_JSON__", program_workspace_json)
    )

async function runtimeSnapshot(browserController) {
  const snapshot = await browserController.evaluate(runtimeSnapshotExpression());
  if (!snapshot) {
    throw new Error("window.RUNTIME is not available in the Web client");
  }
  return snapshot;
}

async function dispatchDomPress(browserController, selector) {
  return browserController.evaluate(`(() => {
    const selector = ${JSON.stringify(selector)};
    const element = document.querySelector(selector);
    if (!element) {
      throw new Error("DOM selector not found: " + selector);
    }
    const target = element;
    target.scrollIntoView({ block: "center", inline: "center" });
    const rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    const clientX = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
    const clientY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;
    const pointerDown = new PointerEvent("pointerdown", {
      bubbles: true,
      cancelable: true,
      button: 0,
      buttons: 1,
      pointerId: 1,
      pointerType: "mouse",
      isPrimary: true,
      clientX,
      clientY
    });
    target.dispatchEvent(pointerDown);
    return {
      selector,
      tag: target.tagName,
      disabled: Boolean(target.disabled),
      text: String(target.innerText || target.textContent || "").trim()
    };
  })()`);
}

async function createCdpPage(browserSession, port, timeoutMs) {
  const target = await browserSession.send("Target.createTarget", { url: "about:blank" });
  const targetId = target.targetId;
  const targets = await requestJson(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/json/list`, { timeoutMs: 1000 });
  const pageTarget = Array.isArray(targets.payload)
    ? targets.payload.find((item) => item.id === targetId)
    : null;
  if (!pageTarget || !pageTarget.webSocketDebuggerUrl) {
    throw new Error(`Could not find CDP target ${targetId}`);
  }
  const pageSession = await CdpSession.connect(pageTarget.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
  await pageSession.send("Runtime.enable");
  await pageSession.send("Page.enable");
  return {
    navigate: async (url) => {
      const loadPromise = pageSession.waitForEvent("Page.loadEventFired", timeoutMs);
      await pageSession.send("Page.navigate", { url });
      await loadPromise;
    },
    evaluate: async (expression) => {
      const result = await pageSession.send("Runtime.evaluate", {
        expression,
        awaitPromise: true,
        returnByValue: true,
      });
      if (result.exceptionDetails) {
        throw new Error(`CDP evaluation failed: ${JSON.stringify(result.exceptionDetails)}`);
      }
      return result.result ? result.result.value : undefined;
    },
    waitFor: async (expression, waitTimeoutMs, label) => {
      const deadline = Date.now() + waitTimeoutMs;
      let lastValue = undefined;
      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        lastValue = await pageSession.send("Runtime.evaluate", {
          expression: `Boolean(${expression})`,
          awaitPromise: true,
          returnByValue: true,
        });
        if (lastValue.result && lastValue.result.value === true) {
          return;

"""Web runtime JavaScript fragment: utilities."""

from __future__ import annotations

RUNTIME_UTILITIES_JS = r"""  function ok(message, code, details) {
    return { accepted: true, status: "accepted", message, code: code || "ok", details: details || {} };
  }

  function fail(message, code, details) {
    return { accepted: false, status: "rejected", message, code: code || message || "runtime.command_rejected", details: details || {} };
  }

  function numberOr(value, fallback) {
    const numeric = Number(value);
    return Number.isFinite(numeric) ? numeric : fallback;
  }

  function roundTo(value, digits) {
    const factor = 10 ** digits;
    return Math.round(numberOr(value, 0) * factor) / factor;
  }

  function formatDuration(totalSeconds) {
    const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(totalSeconds));
    const hours = String(Math.floor(seconds / 3600)).padStart(2, "0");
    const minutes = String(Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60)).padStart(2, "0");
    const secs = String(seconds % 60).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${hours}:${minutes}:${secs}`;
  }

  function cloneValue(value) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) {
      return value;
    }
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
  }
}

function enrichWorkOffsetActivationPayload(args) {
  const payload = { ...(args || {}) };
  const row = selectedWorkOffsetRow(payload.selector || payload.g_code);
  if (!row) {
    return payload;
  }
  const selector = String(row.g_code || row.target_ref || payload.selector || payload.g_code || "").trim();
  if (selector) {
    payload.selector = selector;
  }
  if (row.g_code) {
    payload.g_code = row.g_code;
  }
  const revision = Number(row.active_state_revision);
  if (Number.isFinite(revision)) {
    payload.expected_revision = revision;
  }
  return payload;
}

function isWorkOffsetRevisionMismatch(result) {
  if (!result || result.accepted !== false) {
    return false;
  }
  const code = String(result.code || "");
  const message = String(result.message || "");
  return code === "work_offset.revision_mismatch"
    || code === "work_offset.active_revision_mismatch"
    || code === "work_offset.expected_revision_required"
    || code === "wcs_revision_mismatch"
    || message.includes("work offset table changed");
}

function workOffsetRevisionMismatchNotice(result) {
  const details = result && result.details ? result.details : {};
  const current = Number(details.current_revision);
  const expected = Number(details.expected_revision);
  if (Number.isFinite(current) && Number.isFinite(expected)) {
    return `Work offset table changed; refresh and retry (current ${current}, expected ${expected})`;
  }
  return "Work offset table changed; refresh and retry";
}

function handleWorkOffsetCommandResult(result, fallbackMessage) {
  if (!result || result.accepted !== false) {
    return false;
  }
  const message = isWorkOffsetRevisionMismatch(result)
    ? workOffsetRevisionMismatchNotice(result)
    : String(result.message || fallbackMessage || "Work offset command rejected");
  writeLocalNotice(message);
  return true;
}

function invokeRefreshWorkOffsetTable() {
  const result = invokeRuntimeCommandWithResult("wcs.commands.refresh_offset_table", {
    known_revision: currentWorkOffsetSnapshotRevisionForCommand(),
  });
  Promise.resolve(result).then((resolved) => {
    if (resolved && resolved.accepted) {
      writeLocalNotice("Work offset table refreshed");
    } else if (resolved) {
      handleWorkOffsetCommandResult(resolved, "Work offset refresh rejected");
    }
  });
  return result;
}

function nextToolDraftId() {
  const rows = Array.isArray(resourceValue("tooling.tool.table", [])) ? resourceValue("tooling.tool.table", []) : [];
  let number = 100;
  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const value = Number(row && row.t_no);
    if (Number.isFinite(value)) {
      number = Math.max(number, value + 100);
    }
  });
  return `T${String(number).padStart(4, "0")}`;

function parseToolRecordDraft(value) {
  const parts = String(value || "").split(",").map((part) => part.trim());
  const toolId = parts[0] || "";
  if (!toolId) {
    return null;
  }
  const payload = { tool_id: toolId };
  if (parts[1]) payload.tool_number = parts[1];
  if (parts[2]) payload.name = parts[2];
  if (parts[3]) payload.tool_type = parts[3];
  return payload;
}

function normalizeToolSelectorText(value, prefix) {
  const text = String(value || "").trim();
  const marker = String(prefix || "").toLowerCase();
  if (text.length > 1 && text[0].toLowerCase() === marker && /^[0-9]/.test(text.slice(1))) {
    return text.slice(1);
  }
  return text;
}

function toolDetailText(value) {
  return value === undefined || value === null ? "" : String(value);
}

function toolOffsetDetailMode() {
  const mode = String(RUNTIME.readLocalState("runtime_state.tool_offset_detail_mode") || "view");
  return mode === "create" ? "create_tool" : mode;
}

function isToolOffsetCreateMode(mode) {
  const normalized = String(mode || toolOffsetDetailMode());
  return normalized === "create_tool" || normalized === "create_edge" || normalized === "create_tool_at_pocket";
}

function isToolOffsetEditMode(mode) {
  return String(mode || toolOffsetDetailMode()) === "edit";
}

      const text = staleToolingTable
        ? "Tool offset table changed; refresh and retry"
        : message || [path, status].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
      if (!text || Object.is(localState["runtime_state.last_notice"], text)) {
        return false;
      }
      localState["runtime_state.last_notice"] = text;
      return true;
    }

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
WEB_SCENARIO_ROOT = ROOT / "tests" / "ui_scenarios" / "web"
QML_SMOKE_ROOT = ROOT / "tests" / "qml_smoke"
WEB_RUNNER = ROOT / "tools" / "ui_automation" / "run_web_ui_scenario.js"
UI_SMOKE_RUNNER = ROOT / "tools" / "run_ui_automation_smoke.sh"
QML_BUTTONS = ROOT / "client" / "qml_client" / "widget_fragments" / "buttons.py"
QML_SMOKE_HELPERS = ROOT / "client" / "qml_client" / "main_qml_parts" / "smoke_testing.py"
SUMMARY = ROOT / "docs" / "SUMMARY.md"


class UiAutomationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_web_ui_runner_exists(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(WEB_RUNNER.exists(), WEB_RUNNER)
        text = WEB_RUNNER.read_text(encoding="utf8")
        self.assertIn("startNativeServer", text)
        self.assertIn("dispatchDomPointer", text)
        self.assertIn("expectDom", text)

function readNestedValue(value, pathSuffix) {
  let current = value;
  for (const part of String(pathSuffix || "").split(".")) {
    if (!part) {
      continue;
    }
    if (current === null || current === undefined || typeof current !== "object") {
      return undefined;
    }
    current = current[part];
  }
  return current;
}

function matchesExpectedValue(actual, expected) {
  if (expected && typeof expected === "object" && !Array.isArray(expected)) {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "one_of")) {
      return Array.isArray(expected.one_of)
        && expected.one_of.some((candidate) => matchesExpectedValue(actual, candidate));
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "not_equals")) {
      return JSON.stringify(actual) !== JSON.stringify(expected.not_equals);
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "contains")) {
      return String(actual || "").includes(String(expected.contains));
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "array_contains")) {
      return Array.isArray(actual)
        && actual.some((candidate) => matchesObjectSubset(candidate, expected.array_contains));
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "not_array_contains")) {
      return !Array.isArray(actual)
        || !actual.some((candidate) => matchesObjectSubset(candidate, expected.not_array_contains));
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "length_at_least")) {
      const length = actual && typeof actual.length === "number" ? actual.length : -1;
      return length >= Number(expected.length_at_least || 0);
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "not_contains")) {
      return !String(actual || "").includes(String(expected.not_contains));
    }
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(expected, "truthy")) {
      return Boolean(actual) === Boolean(expected.truthy);
    }
  }
  return JSON.stringify(actual) === JSON.stringify(expected);
}

function matchesObjectSubset(actual, expected) {
  if (expected && typeof expected === "object" && !Array.isArray(expected)) {
    if (!actual || typeof actual !== "object") {
      return false;
    }

ensure_metanc_real_server() {
  if ! metanc_real_sources_require_rebuild; then
    return
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "${HMI_DIR}/../tooling_management" ]]; then
    echo "tooling_management source is not available at ${HMI_DIR}/../tooling_management." >&2
    echo "Run this helper from a MetaNC checkout, or sync nrt/hmi into MetaNC first." >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  if [[ ! -d "${HMI_DIR}/../work_offset" ]]; then
    echo "work_offset source is not available at ${HMI_DIR}/../work_offset." >&2
    echo "Run this helper from a MetaNC checkout with nrt/work_offset available." >&2
    exit 1
  fi

  local cmake_args=(
    -S "${HMI_DIR}/server"
    -B "${BUILD_DIR}"
    -DHMI_TOOLING_BACKEND=tooling_management
    -DHMI_TOOLING_MANAGEMENT_DIR="${HMI_DIR}/../tooling_management"
    -DHMI_WORK_OFFSET_BACKEND=work_offset
    -DHMI_WORK_OFFSET_DIR="${HMI_DIR}/../work_offset"
    -DTOOLING_MANAGEMENT_WITH_AMENT=OFF
    -DTOOLING_MANAGEMENT_ENABLE_SQLITE=ON
    -DWORK_OFFSET_WITH_AMENT=OFF
    -DWORK_OFFSET_ENABLE_SQLITE=ON
  )
  local toolchain_file

+def _reject_duplicate_json_keys(pairs: list[tuple[str, object]]) -> dict[str, object]:
+    result: dict[str, object] = {}
+    for key, value in pairs:
+        if key in result:
+            raise ValueError(f"duplicate JSON key: {key}")
+        result[key] = value
+    return result
+

+def _load_scenario(path: Path) -> dict[str, object]:

# Generated Distribution

This directory packages the split runtime artifacts produced from the HMI DSL source package.

Contents:

- `client/web/`: generated Web thin client
- `client/qml/`: built Qt/QML client and packaged program workspace
- `server/runtime_contract_bundle.json`: packaged server input contract bundle
- `server/native/server`: packaged native Drogon server binary
- `server/native/lib/`: packaged Drogon runtime libraries when needed
- `contract/`: exported runtime contract artifacts
- `run_client_qml.sh`: launch helper for the packaged QML executable
- `run_split_qml.sh`: launch helper for the packaged QML client with the repo fixture mock server
- `run_split_qml_native.sh`: launch helper for the packaged QML client with the packaged native server
- `run_client_web.sh`: launch helper for the packaged Web client only
- `run_server_fixture.sh`: launch helper for the repo fixture mock server only
- `run_server_native.sh`: launch helper for the packaged native server
- `run_server_tooling_management.sh`: launch helper for a native server built against MetaNC `tooling_management`
- `run_server_metanc_real.sh`: launch helper for one native server built against real `tooling_management` and real `work_offset`
- `run_seed_tooling_management_demo.sh`: seed demo tool records through the HMI runtime API
- `run_split_web.sh`: launch helper for the packaged Web prototype with the repo fixture mock server
- `run_split_web_native.sh`: launch helper for the packaged Web client with the packaged native server
- `run_split_web_tooling_management.sh`: launch helper for the Web client with the real `tooling_management` backend
- `run_split_qml_tooling_management.sh`: launch helper for the QML client with the real `tooling_management` backend
- `run_split_web_metanc_real.sh`: launch helper for the Web client with real `tooling_management` and re<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="668" />tools into the SQLite store.
Set `HMI_TOOLING_MANAGEMENT_REBUILD=1` to force the private MetaNC tooling
server target to rebuild before launch.

The MetaNC real combined launchers keep tooling and WorkOffset storage separate.
Use `HMI_TOOLING_STORE_PATH` for `tooling_management` and
`HMI_WORK_OFFSET_STORE_PATH` for `work_offset`. WorkOffset defaults to
`HMI_WORK_OFFSET_SEED_MODE=preserve`; set `seed-if-empty` plus an explicit
`HMI_WORK_OFFSET_SEED_SNAPSHOT` for a first demo run.

For containers, bind the native server to every interface:

```bash
HMI_SERVER_HOST=0.0.0.0 ./run_server_native.sh 8010
```

Enable SQLite-backed runtime logs:

```bash
HMI_PERSISTENCE_BACKEND=sqlite HMI_RUNTIME_DATA_DIR=runtime-data ./run_server_native.sh 8010
```

Run the split Web preview with the repo fixture mock server:

```bash
./run_split_web.sh
```

Then open:

- `http://127.0.0.1:8010/`

Run the split Web preview with the packaged native server:

```bash
./run_split_web_native.sh
```

Then open:

- `http://127.0.0.1:8000/?server=http://127.0.0.1:8010/api/runtime`

Run the split Web preview with the real MetaNC `tooling_management` backend:

```bash
./run_split_web_tooling_management.sh
```

Run the split Web preview with both real MetaNC backends:

```bash
HMI_WORK_OFFSET_SEED_MODE=seed-if-empty \
HMI_WORK_OFFSET_SEED_SNAPSHOT=../../../work_offset/test/fixtures/snapshots/valid/basic_xyzac.snapshot.json \
./run_split_web_metanc_real.sh 8010 8000
```

Use tooling_management's SQLite store-backed runtime for Web restart survival:

```bash
HMI_TOOLING_STORE_KIND=sqlite HMI_TOOLING_STORE_PATH=runtime-data/tooling.sqlite ./run_split_web_tooling_management.sh 8010 8000
```

Then open:

- `http://127.0.0.1:8000/?server=http://127.0.0.1:8010/api/runtime`

## Generated Cleanup Rule

The scripts listed above are the current generated launcher set. Do not add
one-off launcher aliases directly under this directory; update
`tools/generate_targets.sh` and regenerate instead.

    def test_server_can_boot_from_exported_contract_bundle(self) -> None:
        bundle = build_runtime_contract_bundle(load_package(MANIFEST))
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            bundle_path = Path(temp_dir) / "runtime_contract_bundle.json"
            bundle_path.write_text(json.dumps(bundle, indent=2, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")

    def test_wcs_extended_selector_activation_marks_matching_row_active(self) -> None:
        server = MockRuntimeServer(MANIFEST)

        activated = server.invoke_command(
            "wcs.commands.activate", {"selector": "G54.1P1", "expected_revision": 1}
        )
        self.assertTrue(activated["accepted"])
        rows_by_code = {
            row["g_code"]: row for row in activated["state"]["resources"]["wcs.offset.table"]
        }
        self.assertFalse(rows_by_code["G54"]["active"])
        self.assertTrue(rows_by_code["G54.1P1"]["active"])
        active_rows = [
            row for row in activated["state"]["resources"]["wcs.offset.table"]
            if row["active"]
        ]
        self.assertEqual([row["g_code"] for row in active_rows], ["G54.1P1"])

2. If request `axis_id` is supplied, require it to match
   `component_axis_ids[field]`.
3. Reject the edit before calling WorkOffset when the field has no mapped
   `axis_id`.
4. Resolve `target_ref` when supplied; otherwise use the row identity mapped
   from `g_code` in the current HMI table.
5. Call `set_frame_component` with `component_kind=coarse_translation` and an
   HMI/operator `source`.
6. On accepted result, query `settable_table` again.
7. Publish refreshed canonical `wcs.offset.table`.
8. Return accepted status and command-specific details; refreshed table metadata
   comes from the post-command resource poll or an explicit
   `wcs.commands.refresh_offset_table`.

### `wcs.commands.refresh_offset_table`

Request:

| Field | Type | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `known_revision` | optional int | table snapshot revision already observed by the client |

Response:

| Field | Type | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `accepted` | bool | command acceptance status |
| `message` | string | operator-facing summary |
| `snapshot_revision` | int | current table snapshot revision |
| `row_count` | int | visible row count in the refreshed table |
| `changed` | bool | whether the table revision differs from `known_revision`, or true when no known revision was supplied |
| `diagnostics` | string | backend diagnostics suitable for HMI logs |

Behavior:

1. Fetch the current WorkOffset table snapshot without mutating controller state.
2. Compare `known_revision`, when present, with the table `snapshot_revision`
   from `WorkOffsetViewResult.snapshot_revision`.
3. Return `changed=true` when the caller had no known revision or the table
   revision changed.
4. Publish refreshed canonical `wcs.offset.table`; keep `tooling.wcs.table` as a
   synchronized legacy alias.
5. Return unavailable diagnostics instead of fabricating rows when the WorkOffset
   backend cannot provide a table.

<<<<<<< HEAD
=======
When the backend is unavailable, the HMI resource may publish an empty
`wcs.offset.table` only together with `wcs.offset.table.diagnostic` and rejected
or unavailable command status. Consumers must treat that as a failure envelope,
not as an accepted empty WorkOffset domain projection.

>>>>>>> 4e7d80c0 (feat(hmi): finalize WorkOffset table integration)
`snapshot_revision` is the table snapshot/version field. It must not be derived
from `ActiveWorkOffsetState.revision`, which is the activation-state concurrency
token used by `wcs.commands.activate`.

The server treats request `axis_id` as a client echo of the row metadata, not as
an authority. A mismatch between request `axis_id` and the current server row
projection is rejected before calling WorkOffset.

WorkOffset command handlers publish only canonical `wcs.offset.table`. The
application/resource layer synchronizes the legacy `tooling.wcs.table` alias
from that canonical resource.

V1 does not expose fine translation, rotation, scale, or mirror writes through
the table command. Those require a later Details page.

## Stale Writes

Every WorkOffset write should include the correct `expected_revision` source

# Changelog

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- Work Offset Slice 3.5 now has real-backend closure coverage for missing
  activation revisions, invalid revision payloads, unsupported WorkOffset seed
  modes, backend-unavailable diagnostics, and the combined
  `tooling_management` plus WorkOffset real-backend CTest gate.
- Work Offset Slice 4 now implements the strict two-level PARAM softkey
  hierarchy and generated Web/QML zero-offset table behavior: the Work Offset
  footer owns activation and refresh, activation/edit commands are enriched
  from row metadata, stale writes show a WorkOffset-specific refresh/retry
  notice, and Web/QML smoke covers positive edit plus stale no-mutation paths.
- Manual operation now has a simulator-backed SINUMERIK-style `REF POINT`
  slice under JOG. The retained contract adds `runtime_state.jog_submode`,
  `jog.commands.set_submode`, `jog.commands.start_reference_return`,
  `jog.commands.cancel_reference_return`, and per-axis
  `axis.*.reference_state`; Web, QML, mock runtime, and the native simulator
  share deterministic reference-return behavior and targeted automation.
- Shared Web/QML parity coverage now includes
  `tests/parity_scenarios/rejected_command_notice.json`, a WebSocket-only
  rejected-command scenario that verifies operator/command notices still reach
  the client footer when there is no state-change fallback. The scenario is
  consumed by mock-server validation, browser-backed Web runtime smoke, and the
  new QML strict smoke `runtime_strict_rejected_command_notice.js`.
- Web UI automation now has a packaged-distribution scenario runner under
  `tools/ui_automation/run_web_ui_scenario.js` plus the first browser-driven

## Slice 3.5 Spec Closure

This checkpoint closes policy cells that remained implicit after Slice 3. It is
not a new WorkOffset domain feature.

| Item | Required evidence | Current status |
|---|---|---|
| missing activation revision policy | real-backend test or contract note proving missing `expected_revision` is rejected in real mode | passed: `work_offset_real_backend_test` covers `work_offset.expected_revision_required` and no active-row mutation |
| invalid revision payloads | adapter or command parser test for non-integer / non-finite revision values | passed: `work_offset_runtime_extension_adapter_test` rejects quoted, negative, and non-finite `expected_revision` as `work_offset.expected_revision_invalid` |
| missing axis mapping | client/server test proving no command is submitted or accepted without `component_axis_ids[field]` | passed server side: `work_offset_real_backend_test` rejects unknown/missing projected fields before mutation; client suppression remains Slice 4 |
| unsupported store mode | startup failure evidence or explicit V1 non-support note | passed: `work_offset_real_backend_test` rejects unsupported `HMI_WORK_OFFSET_SEED_MODE`; real WorkOffset V1 uses SQLite only |
| backend unavailable | rejected diagnostic evidence without accepted empty real table | passed: `work_offset_runtime_extension_adapter_test` returns `work_offset.backend_unavailable` and publishes `wcs.offset.table.diagnostic` |
| mock/real facade parity | parity test or documented allowed differences | passed: mock/real share row identity, revisions, axis mapping, stale and missing-revision rejection, aliasing, and no optimistic mutation |
| combined real-backend CTest | `ctest --test-dir nrt/hmi/generated/server-build-metanc-real --output-on-failure` | passed: 15 tests, after reconfigure/build and REST test changed to read current activation revision <truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2043" />ojection.
